# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-25T04:06:50+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

## Wider reading

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 19:00:00 -0300 — Flamengo: engenheiros são condenados em 2ª instância por incêndio
  A Terceira Câmara Criminal do Tribunal de Justiça do Estado do Rio de Janeiro (TJRJ) condenou, por unanimidade, nesta terça-feira (24), dois engenheiros da empresa NHJ pelo incêndio no Ninho do Urubu , Centro de Treinamento do Flamengo, em Vargem Grande, zona sudoeste do Rio, que matou dez adolescentes em 2019. A decisão acolheu parcialmente o recurso do Ministério Público do Rio de Janeiro (MPRJ) contra a sentença de primeira instância que absolveu os sete acusados. Danilo da Silva Duarte, engenheiro de produção, e Weslley Gimenes, engenheiro civil, foram condenados a oito anos de detenção, cada um, em regime inicial semiaberto. Notícias relacionadas: Moraes nega diminuir pena de Carla Zambelli. Jurista Fabio Konder Comparato morre aos 90 anos. Justiça marca audiência sobre morte de médic
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/justica/noticia/2026-11/ninho-do-urubu-engenheiros-sao-condenados-em-2a-instancia-por-incendio
- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 18:47:00 -0300 — Mineradora é multada em R$ 16,9 milhões no Pará por poluição de rio
  Após denúncias de moradores dos municípios de Tucumã e São Félix do Xingu, no sudeste do Pará, a empresa Mineração Caraíba S.A., conhecida como Ero Brasil, recebeu duas multas do governo estadual em razão da poluição de rios das região. As multas somam R$ 16, 9 milhões. A Secretaria de Estado de Meio Ambiente, Clima e Sustentabilidade (Semas) do Pará informou nesta terça-feira (24) que também interditou a extração e o beneficiamento de minério de cobre em Tucumã. Seguindo a Semas, as equipes de fiscalização receberam materiais de moradores, com vídeos sobre a situação dos rios da região que mostravam mortandade de peixes e animais, além de mudança na turbidez da água.   https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/meio-ambiente/noticia/2026-11/mineradora-e-multada-em-r-169-milhoes-no-para-por-poluicao-de-rio

As planned, the mission lasted a total of 2 hours and 4 minutes from lift-off to the separation of the last satellites. This tenth Ariane 6 mission is clear evidence of the industrial ramp-up now under way.   The number of Koreans who moved to new residences rose slightly in October from a year earlier due to a low base effect, but the downside trend continued, government data showed Wednesday. Around 453,000 people changed residences to other administrative districts in October, up 2.6 percent, or 11,000, from a year earlier, according to the monthly tally from the Ministry of Data and Statistics. The ministry attributed the on-year increase to a base effect as the number fell sharply in October last year amid the Chuseok holiday season.   Korea's retail sales rose in October from a year earlier on solid sales from online platforms, government data showed Wednesday. Combined sales of 26 major retail companies increased 5.7 percent from a year earlier, according to data by the Ministry of Trade, Industry and Resources. Offline retailers reported a 1.4 percent decrease in sales due mainly to sluggish sales in large discount outlets as the Chuseok holiday fell in September this year. Last year, the Korean equivalent of Thanksgiving Day was in October based on the lunar calendar.   Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission - ohb.de Skip to content Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission Bremen, November 17, 2026 Low Earth Orbit (LEO) is becoming increasingly congested as the amount of space debris continues to grow. Thousands of defunct satellites and millions of debris fragments orbit Earth, increasing the risk of collisions. With the ClearSpace-1 mission (CS-1), Europe aims to demonstrate for the first time how a satellite can be captured and safely removed from orbit in a controlled manner. For this pioneering mission, the European Space Agency (ESA) has selected OHB System AG as prime contractor.   Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data HOME Publications Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data Austin Stone November 23, 2026 • 3 min read PlanetScope image of the Everglades coastline in Florida, captured May 15, 2025. © 2026, Planet Labs PBC. All Rights reserved. Publications Publications Satellite imagery is a critical resource in monitoring the health of coastal environments that are increasingly vulnerable to the impacts of climate and land use change. However, these ecosystems are so dynamic that many short-lived events can be missed between image captures by satellites that provide resolutions coarser than 10 meters with multiday revisit times, such as Landsat and Sentinel-2. While high-resolution aerial flights or drone surveys offer sub-meter-scale detail, they are costly, localized, and impossible to deploy systematically across entire national coas
  https://www.planet.com/pulse/monitoring-the-coasts-with-near-daily-satellite-data

Back ‘Trust Me, It’s Gonna Take Off’: Inside Electron’s First 100 Launches Share this Rocket Lab team members at Launch Complex 1 in Māhia, New Zealand, celebrate Electron’s 100th launch. By Matt McKinney It started with about 10 people, a carbon-fiber rocket that skeptics said would never work, and a sheep farm at the end of a windswept New Zealand peninsula. On Nov. 13, 2026, Electron flew for the 100 th time , reaching the mark faster than any other commercially developed rocket in history. Electron, which took roughly four years to develop, now rolls off the factory floor every eight days. Even by today's fast-moving aerospace standards, few small launch vehicles have reached even double-digi

  China’s latest crewed space mission, Shenzhou-24, lifted off on Wednesday morning, taking three astronauts – including the first foreign crew member – to the Tiangong space station.   An American soldier who sold what he claimed was top secret know-how to Chinese-linked buyers faces up to a decade in prison after admitting passing on sensitive military information. What would happen if an asteroid the size of the Great Pyramid of Giza was on a collision course with Earth? Its impact could destroy entire cities, or generate a devastating tsunami if it struck the ocean. Would humanity be prepared to respond to such a threat? That is precisely what the European Space Agency's Hera mission aims to help determine. His previous plays [For Black Boys Who Have Considered Suicide When the Hue Gets Too Heavy](https://www.theguardian.com/stage/2023/apr/02/for-black-boys-review-apollo-theatre-london) and [Retrograde](https://www.theguardian.com/stage/2023/apr/27/retrograde-review-sidney-poitier-kiln-theatre-london) both streaked their way into the West End. This new one is filled with mighty ambition but does not lift off in the same way. It is based on what he calls a “true(ish)” story. The figure of Edward Mukuka Nkoloso (Lucian Msamati) is taken from Zambian history. - undated — CDE y Pdte. Franco tendrán saneamiento para 300.000 habitantes con inversión de USD 24 millones
  La Nación / CDE y Pdte. Franco tendrán saneamiento para 300.000 habitantes con inversión de USD 24 millones Unas 300.000 personas de Ciudad del Este y Presidente Franco serán beneficiadas con un nuevo sistema integral de saneamiento, cuya construcción demandará una inversión de USD 24 millones. El proyecto contempla la instalación de redes de alcantarillado sanitario y la construcción de una planta de tratamiento de aguas residuales en Alto Paraná, informó este martes la agencia IP. La obra fue adjudicada por el Ministerio de Obras Públicas y Comunicaciones (MOPC) a la empresa española Acciona, que tendrá a su cargo el diseño y la ejecución de las distintas etapas del emprendimiento. El proyecto incluye: la instalación de colectores redes de alcantarillado estaciones de bombeo la construcción, equipamiento y puesta en funcionamiento de la Planta de Tratamiento de Aguas Residuales (PTAR)
  https://www.lanacion.com.py/negocios/2026/11/24/cde-y-pdte-franco-tendran-saneamiento-para-300000-habitantes-con-inversion-de-usd-24-millones/

- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 19:00:00 -0300 — Flamengo: engenheiros são condenados em 2ª instância por incêndio
  A Terceira Câmara Criminal do Tribunal de Justiça do Estado do Rio de Janeiro (TJRJ) condenou, por unanimidade, nesta terça-feira (24), dois engenheiros da empresa NHJ pelo incêndio no Ninho do Urubu , Centro de Treinamento do Flamengo, em Vargem Grande, zona sudoeste do Rio, que matou dez adolescentes em 2019. A decisão acolheu parcialmente o recurso do Ministério Público do Rio de Janeiro (MPRJ) contra a sentença de primeira instância que absolveu os sete acusados. Danilo da Silva Duarte, engenheiro de produção, e Weslley Gimenes, engenheiro civil, foram condenados a oito anos de detenção, cada um, em regime inicial semiaberto. As multas somam R$ 16, 9 milhões. A Secretaria de Estado de Meio Ambiente, Clima e Sustentabilidade (Semas) do Pará informou nesta terça-feira (24) que também interditou a extração e o beneficiamento de minério de cobre em Tucumã. Seguindo a Semas, as equipes de fiscalização receberam materiais de moradores, com vídeos sobre a situação dos rios da região que mostravam mortandade de peixes e animais, além de mudança na turbidez da água. - Tue, 24 Nov 2026 05:00:00 GMT — Amendment of Class D Airspace and Class E Airspace Over Tri-Cities, TN
  This action amends Class D and Class E airspace over Tri- Cities, TN. Specifically, this action modifies the Tri-Cities Class D and Class E surface area airspace by expanding the radius and lateral boundaries to the northeast and removing the Tri-Cities Class E surface area extension airspace to the northeast. Additionally, this action modifies the Tri-Cities Class E transitional airspace beginning at 700 feet above the surface by expanding the lateral boundaries of the western portion of the airspace. Lastly, this action updates the airport name within the Class D and E legal descriptions and other outdated verbiage.
  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/24/2026-23948/amendment-of-class-d-airspace-and-class-e-airspace-over-tri-cities-tn

- undated — Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification
  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms. The dataset comes with an accompanying &#8220;answer key&#8221; that lists the conjunctions in the dataset &#8211; providing a voluntary tool for self-assessment. DISCLAIMER : While this ephemeris dataset was crafted to include stressing edge cases in addition to an operational catalog, it is not completely comprehensive (as there are nearly infinite combinations of orbits and conjunction geometries possible).   https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/

  TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies &amp; Service Comparison &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background Space debris and collision avoidance are challenges for all spacecraft, regardless of their nation of registry. As laid out in the Office of Space Commerce’s&nbsp; Vision for Global SSA Coordination , international collaboration is key to enhancing data consistency, coverage, and transparency. Both the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and the European Union Space Surveillance and Tracking (EU SST), in partnership with the commercial space situational awareness (SSA) industry, will offer free space SSA services to spacecraft operators worldwide to prevent in-orbit collisions. - undated — Quantum Space Goes Public as SPACs Return
  Quantum Space Goes Public as SPACs Return Skip to content Quantum Space is the latest space company to go public this year, closing its merger with special purpose acquisition company (SPAC) Inflection Point Acquisition Corp. VI on Monday. The combined company is expected to start trading on the NYSE today under the ticker $QSPC. The deal brought in ~$300M of new capital at an implied pro forma equity value of ~$958M, down from the ~$1.2B Quantum predicted when it announced the merger in June. Quantum plans to pour the cash into production of its Ranger and Scout spacecraft, though Ranger has yet to fly.&nbsp; In June, CEO Jim Bridenstine said in a press conference that speed drove the choice of a SPAC over a traditional IPO. &#8220;We need to go as fast as possible to get these capabilities on-orbit as soon as possible,&#8221; he said.&nbsp; SPAC is back: For years, the word SPAC alone
  https://payloadspace.com/quantum-space-goes-public-as-spacs-return/
- undated — Report Urges Congress to Designate Space as Critical Infrastructure
  Report Urges Congress to Designate Space as Critical Infrastructure Skip to content A think tank is urging Congress to act to protect critical infrastructure for space operations in a new report released today. The Foundation for Defense of Democracies argued that space is critical to the American way of life, from PNT to weather forecasting to communications—and that interruption of these services would be detrimental to national security. However, policy to designate space as critical infrastructure in the same way as the electric grid or financial systems has lagged behind this technical reality.&nbsp; The threats: Adversaries, including China and Russia, are already hard at work developing and testing tech to deny US entities access to space, through nonkinetic tactics such as jamming and spoofing as well as physical assaults such as anti-satellite missiles or maneuvering of spacecra
  https://payloadspace.com/report-urges-congress-to-designate-space-as-critical-infrastructure/
- undated — Exclusive: Verde Technologies Will Test Perovskite Solar Cells on the ISS in 2027
  Exclusive: Verde Technologies Will Test Perovskite Solar Cells on the ISS in 2027 Skip to content Solar cell manufacturer Verde Technologies signed a contract to send its lightweight, ultra-thin perovskite solar cells to the ISS NET June, company officials told Payload. The mission—part of NASA’s Materials International Space Station Experiment (MISSE) , which focuses on testing new materials and components on the orbital lab—will involve bolting Verde’s solar cells to the outside of the ISS for six months before they’re returned to Earth for analysis. The research aims to validate Verde’s perovskite solar cell technology in space for the first time and open the door for Verde to begin producing and selling solar cells to spacecraft operators at scale. “The plan for us is to validate the technology completely, then start entering the market in steps,” CEO Jean-Noel Poirier told Payload.
  https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-verde-technologies-will-test-perovskite-solar-cells-on-the-iss-in-2027/

- undated — First Pakistani Astronaut Enroute to Tiangong
  First Pakistani Astronaut Enroute to Tiangong &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com China launched a new crew to the Tiangong space station this evening EST. Joining two Chinese taikonauts is Pakistan&#8217;s first astronaut, Muhammad Zeeshan Ali. This is the first time China has launched a non-Chinese crew member to space. Docking is expected about 3.5 hours after liftoff. Launch of Shenzhou-24 was at 7:18 pm Eastern Standard Time, or 8:18 am November 25 local time at China&#8217;s Jiuquan Satellite Launch Center in Inner Mongolia. Shenzhou-24 lifts off from the Jiuquan Satellite Launch Center in Inner Mongolia Autonomous Region soon after sunrise, 8:18 am November 25, 2026 local time (7:18 pm, November 24 EST). Screenshot from CGTN. China&#8217;s Manned Space Agency and Pakistan&#8217;s national space agency SUPARCO announced an agreement to launch a Pakistani astronaut in February 2025. Two p
  https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/first-pakistani-astronaut-enroute-to-tiangong/
- undated — White House Requests Billions for NASA, Golden Dome in Next CR
  White House Requests Billions for NASA, Golden Dome in Next CR &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com Last night the White House sent Congress a list of funding it wants included in a new Continuing Resolution (CR) that would keep the government open after the current CR expires on December 11. The request includes $2 billion for NASA&#8217;s Artemis program to return astronauts to the Moon and $5 billion for DOD&#8217;s Golden Dome missile defense project. Illustration: SpaceQ. Natural Resources Canada (NRCan) is commissioning a study of what would happen to Canada&#8217;s power grids if Global Positioning System (GPS) signals went down. GPS and similar satellite systems supply the precise time that grid equipment uses to stay in sync, and the study will ask operators whether they want a Canadian-run source of that time. Public Services and Procurement Canada issued the request for proposals on Nov. 13 on behalf of NRCan, which is the lead federal department for the security of critical energy infrastructure. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

#### ESA top news (government, en, feed)

- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 09:00:00 +0100 — Euclid breaks own quasar record by 40 million years
  The European Space Agency’s Euclid space telescope has discovered the most distant quasar to date: a rare 360 million-sun-mass galactic core seen just 630 million years after the Big Bang. The discovery breaks Euclid’s own previous record, announced only a few months ago[1.1], by peering back in time an additional 40 million years. It also raises a compelling question: how could such an enormous black hole have formed and grown so rapidly after the Big Bang?
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/science/euclid/euclid-breaks-own-quasar-record-by-40-million-years
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 19:00:00 +0100 — BepiColombo safely in Mercury orbit despite unexpected challenge
  On 21 November, starting at 18:24 UTC, BepiColombo achieved Mercury orbit insertion (MOI), the second major milestone of its planetary arrival phase. As soon as the signal from the spacecraft was re-established after a scheduled loss of contact, the Mission Control Team at the European Space Operations Centre (ESOC) in Darmstadt, Germany, saw that the spacecraft had entered safe mode. An issue occurred during the thruster burn, causing the manoeuvre to end earlier than expected. Since then, teams at ESOC have been working around the clock with lead spacecraft manufacturer Airbus and colleagues across the joint ESA and Japan Aerospace Exploration Agency (JAXA) mission to investigate the issue, which has been traced to a software glitch. BepiColombo is safe and in a stable orbit around Mercu
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/operations/bepicolombo-safely-in-mercury-orbit-despite-unexpected-challenge
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 15:10:46 +0100 — Hera’s Didymos asteroid in eclipse
  A solar eclipse in deep space, shown in this early view of the Didymos binary asteroids from the European Space Agency’s Hera mission for planetary defence. The smaller Dimorphos moonlet is seen casting its shadow across part of its Didymos parent body, to blank out the Sun across the shaded stretch of rocky surface.

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . Entre sus competencias clave se encuentran asegurar la acreditaci&oacute;n de seguridad para todas las acciones

La AEE tiene el mandato de unificar la postura nacional, aportando coherencia, coordinaci&oacute;n y una visi&oacute;n estrat&eacute;gica integral a la participaci&oacute;n de Espa&ntilde;a en el exterior. - undated — BepiColombo, inserimento in orbita con imprevisto
  BepiColombo, inserimento in orbita con imprevisto &#8211; MEDIA INAF Dopo otto anni e 10,2 miliardi di chilometri percorsi, sabato 21 novembre 2026 la missione dell’Agenzia spaziale europea (Esa) BepiColombo è entrata in orbita attorno a Mercurio. A dare la notizia, un post su X pubblicato dalla stessa agenzia che confermava il completamento della manovra e la ricezione del segnale da parte della sonda. In un post successivo pubblicato il giorno seguente, però, la stessa agenzia ha chiarito che non tutto sarebbe andato come previsto: un&#8217;anomalia software avrebbe provocato l&#8217;interruzione anticipata dell&#8217;accensione dei propulsori, costringendo la sonda a entrare in modalità di sicurezza. In una comunicazione ufficiale pubblicata questa sera, infine, si legge che l’inserimento in orbita è comunque riuscito e che il veicolo si trova in un&#8217;orbita stabile attorno al pia

- undated — Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai
  Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai &#8211; MEDIA INAF Una delle principali sfide dell’esplorazione spaziale dei prossimi decenni è stabilire una presenza umana stabile e duratura sul nostro satellite naturale, un obiettivo a cui punta il programma Artemis della Nasa, che mira anche a gettare le basi per la futura esplorazione di Marte. Affinché ciò sia possibile, sarà tuttavia necessario garantire agli astronauti un approvvigionamento alimentare sostenibile. La permanenza prolungata sulla Luna dipenderà infatti anche dalla possibilità di produrre cibo direttamente sul posto, riducendo la necessità di costose missioni di rifornimento dalla Terra. Patate ottenute negli esperimenti condotti da David Handy e colleghi in un terreno simulante la regolite lunare. #### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

  ## [Een mislukte lancering, een geslaagde landing en heel veel ruimtetelescopen Verder de mijlpaal van één lichtdag van de Voyager 1, een mogelijke nieuwe dwergplaneet - nog zonder naam - en een Chinese missie naar de Jupitermaan Callisto. Dat en meer bespreken Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout en Herbert Blankesteijn in deze nieuwe Space Cowboys. Schrijf je in voor de nieuwsbrief van Space Cowbows en krijg elke aflevering in je mailbox zodra deze uitkomt - op thysroes.nl/spacecowboys SpaceCowboys behandelt ruimtevaart- en astronomienieuws van land, planeet en daarbuiten. Afwisselend gepresenteerd door: Thijs Roes, Herbert Blankesteijn, Michel van Baal, Luc van den Abeelen, Philippe Schoonejans, Erik Laan, Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout, Inge Loes ten Kate, André Kuipers, Bastiaan Bom, Nadine Duursma, Marco Langbroek, Bruno van Wayenburg, Charlotte Pouwels, Marc Heemskerk en Nick Poelstra. - undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/
- undated — 23 november 2026 Wat als…? Betere besluitvorming mogelijk met Digitale tweeling DestinE Lees meer
  Wat als…? Betere besluitvorming mogelijk met Digitale tweeling DestinE - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Zeven jaar en vijfhonderd miljoen euro. Dat investeert de Europese Commissie in de ontwikkeling van de ‘digitale tweeling’ Destination Earth. Maar wat kun je met zo’n tweeling? En hoe onderscheidt hij zich van andere informatiediensten? Daarover ging de eerste nationale DestinE Living Lab dag in Amsterdam. &nbsp; Destination Earth ( DestinE ) is een digitale representatie van onze planeet. Een simulatie die de wisselwerking tussen natuur, klimaat en menselijk handelen op onze planeet zo nauwkeurig mogelijk nabootst. Dat doet hij op basis van verschillende databronnen – waaronder satellietdata van het Europese aardobservatieprogramma Copernicus – en met behulp van geavanceerde klimaat- en weermodellen, supercomputers en AI.&nbsp; ‘Satellietgegevens helpen ons te begrijpe
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nieuws/wat-als-betere-besluitvorming-mogelijk-met-digitale-tweeling-destine/

Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Strom für die Mondbasis: Die US-Raumfahrtbehörde National Aeronautics and Space Administration (NASA) hat die Entwicklung eines Atomkraftwerks ausgeschrieben. Es soll die geplante Mondbasis rund um die Uhr mit Strom versorgen. Weiterlesen nach der Anzeige Lunar Reactor-1 (LR-1) soll eine Leistung von nur 20 Kilowatt liefern – das reicht laut NASA aus, um 16 durchschnittliche US-Haushalte mit Strom zu versorgen. ### LUX

#### Directorate of Defence (defense.gouvernement.lu) (government, en, text-reader)

- undated — Space
  Space offers unprecedented opportunities for development and progress. However, the increasing dependence of our societies on space for their day-to-day functioning also generates vulnerabilities. Like cyber defence, space will gain in importance as a key national security issue. Luxembourg Defence will strive to implement the sector strategy described in the first Defence Space Strategy of 2022. This strategy defines concrete objectives for Defence in order to consolidate Luxembourg's role as a reference partner in the field of space, both nationally and internationally, by strengthening existing capabilities and developing new ones. Luxembourg, having its own space capabilities, can have a significant impact and thus provide its fair share to the overall security and defence effort. Particular attention is being paid to the development of dual-use capabilities and the use of such in accordance with international law. In order to promote responsible behaviour in outer space and in support of the United Kingdom's initiative within the framework of the UN to develop norms of responsible behaviour in outer space, Defence will continue to limit access to Luxembourg's sensitive space t
  https://defense.gouvernement.lu/en/la-defense/space.html

  --> HELLAS–SPACE 2.0: Προκηρύχθηκε ο πρώτος διαγωνισμός ύψους 69 εκατ. ευρώ – Η Ελλάδα αποκτά νέους οπτικούς δορυφόρους πολύ υψηλής ανάλυσης - ertnews.gr Μετάβαση σε περιεχόμενο Στην προκήρυξη του πρώτου διαγωνισμού του Εθνικού Διαστημικού Προγράμματος « HELLAS–SPACE 2.0 », προϋπολογισμού 69 εκατ. ευρώ, προχωρά το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης . Το έργο αφορά στην ανάπτυξη εθνικού αστερισμού τριών οπτικών δορυφόρων Παρατήρησης της Γης πολύ υψηλής χωρικής ανάλυσης, με στόχο την ενίσχυση της αυτονομίας της χώρας στην πρόσβαση σε δορυφορικά δεδομένα και την κάλυψη επιχειρησιακών αναγκών των Δημόσιων Υπηρεσιών. Ο διαγωνισμός διενεργείται από τον Ευρωπαϊκό Οργανισμό Διαστήματος (ESA), σε συνεργασία με το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης και το Ελληνικό Κέντρο Διαστήματος (ΕΛΚΕΔ). Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. - undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate

  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. - undated — Przechwycony Starship nie poleci już w kosmos
  Przechwycony Starship nie poleci już w kosmos Menu Ship 42 uczestniczył w piętnastym locie testowym systemu Starship/Super Heavy. Około 22 godzin po starcie górny stopień powrócił do Starbase, gdzie został przechwycony przez mechaniczne ramiona wieży startowej Mechazilla. Było to pierwsze w historii lotów kosmicznych udane przechwycenie górnego stopnia rakiety nośnej bezpośrednio przez wieżę startową . SpaceX odzyskało drugi stopień Starshipa Warto zauważyć, że Starship był w stanie wyhamować z prędkości ponad 26 tys. km/h , przetrwać wejście w atmosferę i zwolnić do zaledwie kilkuset km/h na wysokości około kilometra nad Ziemią. Właśnie wtedy wykonał manewr obrotu do pozycji pionowej, tzw. „flipa”, uruchamiając trzy silniki Raptor, co pozwoliło na jego kompletne wyhamowanie i udane przechwycenie. Mechazilla catches Starship! Sodelujejo lahko podjetja in institucije, ki se ukvarjajo z inovativnimi materiali, tehnologijami, procesi ali informacijskimi rešitvami. Priložnost za sodelovanje z Eso imajo tudi mala in srednja podjetja, kar potrjujejo uspešni projekti podjetij iz Slovenije, ki so to priložnost že izkoristila.   Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje. Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions. By&nbsp; Patrick Alushula Business Reporter Nation Media Group Thank you for reading businessdailyafrica.com Show plans Five insurers have teamed up for a geothermal exploration risk cover that will compensate investors up to $2 million (Sh257.5 million) when they drill and fail to find sufficient output in Kenya, in a move aimed at spurring private investments in the green energy space. ICEA Lion, Mayfair Insurance, Old Mutual, GA Insurance and Kenya Re as a consortium will underwrite the risks with the hope that the cover will provide incentive to potential investors and banks to back such projects.   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3. Establishment of the Agency (1)There is hereby established a state corporation to be known as the Kenya Space Agency.(2)The Agency shall be a body corporate with perpetual succession and a common seal and shall, in its corporate name, be capable of—(a)suing and being sued;(b)taking, purchasing or otherwise acquiring, holding, charging or disposing of movable and immovable property;(c)receiving, investing, borrowing and lending money; and(d)doing or performing any such other thing or act, including entering into such contracts as may be
  https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

- undated — Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial
  Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Sep 25, 2026 Destaques GGPEN e Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos assinam Carta de Intenção para promover colaboração em satélites, observação da Terra, capacitação e inovação. O Gabinete de Gestão do Programa Espacial Angolano (GGPEN) e a Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos (UAE Space Agency) assinaram, esta quinta-feira, 25 de Setembro, em Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, uma Carta de Intenção que estabelece as bases para o reforço da cooperação entre Angola e os Emirados Árabes Unidos no domínio espacial. O documento foi assinado pelo Director-Geral do GGPEN, Dr. Zolana Rui João, e pelo Director-Geral da Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos, Eng. Salem Butti Salem Al Qubaisi, à margem da Africa Space Expo (ASPEX) 2026, onde Angola
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/angola-e-emirados-arabes-unidos-reforcam-cooperacao-no-sector-espacial
- undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate
- undated — Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space
  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos. The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Jeudi, elle rencontrera les opérateurs économiques togolais à Lomé, avec l'appui de la Chambre de commerce et d'industrie du Togo (CCI Togo). Cette visite prolonge un rapprochement engagé depuis le début de l'année. Une convention signée entre la CCI Togo et la Hebei Enterprise and Culture Association a déjà permis deux missions économiques togolaises au Hebei, en mars et en avril. L'objectif est de faciliter les exportations togolaises vers la Chine et d'attirer davantage d'investissements chinois. Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

#### ISRO (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

Home / Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far. Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added.   China’s latest crewed space mission, Shenzhou-24, lifted off on Wednesday morning, taking three astronauts – including the first foreign crew member – to the Tiangong space station. In a sign of the space programme’s increasing diversity, for about a week, the station will be home to four astronauts from mainland China, one from Hong Kong and one from Pakistan. - Wed, 25 Nov 2026 04:02:03 GMT — Population mobility up slightly in October on base effect
  The number of Koreans who moved to new residences rose slightly in October from a year earlier due to a low base effect, but the downside trend continued, government data showed Wednesday. Around 453,000 people changed residences to other administrative districts in October, up 2.6 percent, or 11,000, from a year earlier, according to the monthly tally from the Ministry of Data and Statistics. The ministry attributed the on-year increase to a base effect as the number fell sharply in October last year amid the Chuseok holiday season. This year's Chuseok fell in September based on the lunar calendar that changes from year to year. However, the ministry said the country's population mobility is in a steady decline from a long-term perspective amid a rapidly aging and decreasing population. T
  https://www.koreatimes.co.kr/southkorea/20261125/population-mobility-up-slightly-in-october-on-base-effect?utm_source=rss
- Wed, 25 Nov 2026 04:02:03 GMT — Retail sales rise 5.7% in October on steady online sales
  Korea's retail sales rose in October from a year earlier on solid sales from online platforms, government data showed Wednesday. Combined sales of 26 major retail companies increased 5.7 percent from a year earlier, according to data by the Ministry of Trade, Industry and Resources. Offline retailers reported a 1.4 percent decrease in sales due mainly to sluggish sales in large discount outlets as the Chuseok holiday fell in September this year. Last year, the Korean equivalent of Thanksgiving Day was in October based on the lunar calendar. Revenue at major online retail platforms rose a solid 10.9 percent from a year earlier in October on steady demand for food and delivery services. Online platforms accounted for 60.3 percent of total retail sales in October.

  The Handong International Law School (HILS) and the Handong Institute for Arbitration and Reconciliation (HIAR) have signed a memorandum of understanding with the Korea Mediation Institute (KMI) to expand academic and practical collaboration and enhance students’ mediation skills. Handong Global University said the agreement was signed on Nov. 11 by HILS Dean Lee Hee-eun, HIAR President Shin Eun-joo and KMI President Moon Yong-ho. HILS and HIAR are affiliated with the university. The partnership aims to draw on the expertise and resources of the three institutions to advance mediation education, promote joint research and help develop mediation practices.   중국 우주정거장, 외국인에 첫 개방…우주협력 새 거점 포석 본문 미래&amp;과학 과학 중국 우주정거장, 외국인에 첫 개방…우주협력 새 거점 포석 곽노필 기자 수정 2026-11-25 10:55 펼침 0:00 Your browser does not support the audio element. 곽노필의 미래창 파키스탄 우주비행사 태운 선저우24호 발사 톈궁서 일주일 체류하며 과학실험 뒤 귀환 파키스탄 우주비행사를 포함해 3명의 우주비행사를 태운 중국의 선저우 24호가 25일 오전 창정2F 로켓에 실려 이륙하고 있다. 중국항천과기집단공사 제공 광고 중국 우주정거장 ‘톈궁’이 처음으로 외국인 우주비행사에게 문을 연다. 미국이 주도하는 국제우주정거장(ISS)의 퇴역 이후, 톈궁을 새로운 국제 우주 협력 거점으로 자리매김하려는 움직임의 일환으로 보인다. 미국은 20년이 훨씬 넘어 노후화된 국제우주정거장을 2030년 퇴역시킬 계획이다. 중국유인우주국(CMSA)은 25일 오전 8시18분(한국시각 9시18분) 간쑤성 주취안위성발사센터에서 파키스탄 공군 조종사 출신의 무함마드 지샨 알리와 중국인 우주비행사 2명을 태운 유인우주선 ‘선저우 24호’를 창정2F 로켓에 실어 발사했다. 광고 선저우 24호는 발사 3시간30분 뒤 고도 390km 상공의 우주정거장 톈궁에 도착한다. 2022년 완공된 톈궁에 중국인이 아닌 외국인 우주비행사가 방문하는 것은 이번이 처음이다. 알리는 지난 7개월간 베이징에서 중국어 학습을 포함한 고강도 훈련을 받았다. 알리는 발사 하루 전 기자회견에서 중국어로 “이번 첫 비행은 나 개인뿐만 아니라 2억5000만 파키스탄 국민의 꿈을 대표하는 것”이라고 소감을 말했다. 광고 광고 선저우 우주선 시뮬레이터 안에서 훈련하고 있는 우주비행사들. 맨 오른쪽이 파키스탄 우주비행사 무함마드

  Josh Aoraki explains what made the Beaver Supermoon special | RNZ Skip to main content space 8:51 am today Josh Aoraki explains what made the Beaver Supermoon special From Morning Report , 8:51 am today It may have been worth staying up late, or setting an early alarm, to catch a glimpse of the Moon overnight. November's full moon reached 'peak illumination' just a couple of hours ago, and this one is a super moon - meaning it coincides with the Moon being at its closest point to Earth in its orbit. Known as a Beaver Supermoon, it's the first of three consecutive supermoons, with another due in December. So what made last night's moon so special? Astronomer Josh Aoraki spoke to John Campbell.
